</div></div></a><div class="homepage-stories__center-column-content gutter-bottom"><h2 id="the-fall-classic-is-here">The Fall Classic Is Here!</h2>
<p>After some seven months of play and about 2,500 games,
the MLB prepares to crown its champion. The 2019 World Series will feature
the Houston Astros and the Washington Nationals. The Astros won in 2017,
while the Nationals are making their first appearance in the championship. Who
will win? We’ll soon find out!</p>

</div><div class="homepage-stories__watching-list"><p><a href="/biography/Gari-Melchers"><strong>Artwork stolen by Nazis found in New York museum</strong></a><br/><small>The FBI recovered a painting by American artist Gari Melchers from the Arkell Museum after confirming Nazis had looted it from a Jewish media mogul in 1933. </small></p>
<hr/>
<p><a href="/place/Uluru-Ayers-Rock"><strong>Final climbs on Uluru</strong></a><br/><small>Uluru (also called Ayers Rock),  the world's largest monolith and a sacred site for Australian Aboriginal people, will be closed to climbers.</small></p>
<hr/>
<p><a href="/story/what-is-the-emoluments-clause"><strong>Is the Emoluments Clause "phony"?</strong></a><br/><small>Despite Pres. Donald Trump’s claim that it is, the clause is in the Constitution (Article I, Section 9, Paragraph 8).</small></p>
<hr/>
<p><a href="/explore/space/meet-the-female-explorers/"><strong>First all-female spacewalk</strong></a><br/><small>U.S. astronauts Jessica Meir and Christina Koch made history when they replaced a power controller at the International Space Station. Learn about other female firsts in space.</small></p>

</div></div></a><div class="mosaic-three-columns__content"><h2 id="grab-your-popcorn">Grab Your Popcorn!</h2>
<p>'Tis the season of carved pumpkins, haunted houses, and, perhaps most importantly, scary movies. So, here are six spooky classics to make you scream.</p>

<div class="subtitle">Trophy Hunting: Can It Really Be Justified By “Conservation Benefits”?</div>
